## Day 123 -- six kilderkins down to the basecamp
About 1:45 pm at the cave mouth. Party stripped to clothes (a turn), gear heaped at the mouth, unguarded, nothing about. Rate ruled by the tool bands with no armour: very heavy, 6", so the half-hour trail took an hour. Loads (gp-wt, DM weights): Dave + Brand on Z (1950 + 700), Blartho + Frython on TL (1000 + 450), Torsat + Dellie on XR (1100 + 900), Ista RH 1100, Tristle JS 800, Hask JS 800. Every load within its person's very-heavy ceiling (Dave 2050, Blartho 1250, Torsat and Ista 1150, the rest 1050). Left at the mouth: one Z and the Y. Two-bearer carry is a ruling, no book table; stated when made. Back up unladen at 12" (half an hour), gear taken up again (a turn). About 3:30 pm. Nothing met. The crew on the sledge-way was at work on the slope; Coll knew nothing new from this.

## Day 123 -- expedition 19, back in to area 10
About 3:35 pm, out of the caves clock. Player retcon accepted: two horses and Rowe and Orm came up from the basecamp with the party. They load the Y and Z kilderkins one each (Z 265 lb takes both men to lift), lead them down the half-hour outcrop path at 12", unload, then bring all three horses up to the cave mouth and wait: about eighty minutes in all, nobody armed, nothing about. Party in at (8,25) at round 9, standard formation, Tristle 40 ft ahead; elf passive rolled at both known secret doors on the route.

## Day 123 -- the barrels checked, expedition 19 rounds 10-80
Door (8,25,V) spiked (two spikes, Dave, quiet). 33 barrels by mark: B4 PF1 TL3 Y2 Z2 QS2 LC3 OG2 HU2 JS1 VW1 SD1 RH1 AW2 UT2 MK1 FT1 EJ1 GG1 (marks less the kilderkins, casks and firkins taken). Hoop d6: SLACK TL1 Y1 LC1 OG1 VW1 FT1 GG1 (7), 26 sound. Kilderkin repair ruled ordinary work, no roll: slack hoops driven home with mallet and a spike as drift, one turn each per worker, hammering loud (one extra check per turn, two turns, nothing came). Contents and weights (barrel 460 lb full of liquid; DM estimate cask 100 lb + 36 gal of goods): the party still knows only SD and RH. About 4:55 pm at round 80. Rowe and Orm and the horses back at the cave mouth by now.

## Day 123 -- the last eight kilderkins rolled out, rounds 80-107
SD x2, OG x2, PF x4 rolled to the cave mouth, Tristle 40 ft ahead with the ten-foot pole; eight rollers (everyone else). Left in area 10: VW firkin, three of the four ropes (audit-skip), crosscut saw, 9 sacks, the 33 barrels. Five checks (four extras d6 = 6, 4, 3, 4 with the earlier ones, anchor 90 d6 = 4): none fired. Elf passive rolled at each known secret door. About 5:20 pm. Rowe and Orm with three horses at the cave mouth.

## Day 123 -- the second carry down, and the three back in (round 107 on)
About 5:25 pm at the cave mouth. Six (Torsat, Blartho, Hask, Dellie, Ista, Brand) stripped and left gear, share of the kit, rations and waterskins in the heap. Horses led by Rowe and Orm: SD, SD and one OG (2300, 2300, 2000; 5,000 less saddle capacity, plenty). Teams of two at the very-heavy 6": Blartho + Torsat the other OG (2000), Ista + Hask and Dellie + Brand a PF each (1650, 825 apiece). Six is the most the six can carry (a PF needs a pair; no one alone carries one). Two PF remain at the cave mouth. Arrival at the basecamp about an hour after the start, round 167 on the dungeon clock; the trio's turns in area 10 run alongside. Ista's lantern topped off (Frython carries it): broached flask emptied, one sealed flask opened; 24 turns, gutters round 347. Dave, Tristle (pole), Frython at (8,25) in area 10 at round 114, the door pulled shut behind them, unspiked; elf passive rolled at both known secret doors.

## Day 123 -- the trio out with the gear, rounds 114-131
Gear (three ropes, crosscut saw, nine sacks: Frython) and the VW firkin (Dave, 930 gp-wt): Dave 1912 and Frython 924 before the six kit up, very heavy 6", 300 ft/turn, seventeen rounds from area 10 to the cave mouth. Anchor 120 check silent. Routing slip caught: party.py sent the first move through the locked door (9,21,H); corrected with a place row and re-routed by the known way. About 5:40 pm at the mouth. The six and Rowe and Orm with the horses are not there yet: carriers reached the basecamp about round 167 (about 6:25), unladen back up at 12" about round 197-202 (about 6:55). The trio waits about an hour and a quarter. Booked as if all back: gear returned to the six, their exemption resumed, rations and waterskins back in the common kit.

## Day 123 -- the trio goes down, all come up together (evening)
Player's plan: the trio does not wait at the mouth; goes down with the gear and firkin (6": an hour, about 5:40 to 6:40 pm), leaves them in the lean-to, and the whole party (six carriers, Rowe, Orm, three horses, trio) climbs together; the six's gear is at the mouth and is taken up there. Unladen climb about 40 minutes at Tristle's 9" (pole and kit): arrive about 7:30 pm, gear up about 7:40 pm. No dice: the dungeon clock stays at round 131; nothing wandering on the outcrop. Booking note: the six's kit-up rows written earlier are the net state at the mouth at 7:40. The two PF kilderkins are still at the mouth. Everything else of the party's kit not carried is in the basecamp lean-to: VW firkin, three ropes, saw, nine sacks.

## Day 123 -- the last two PF on the horses, all down to the basecamp
About 7:40 pm at the mouth; PF 1650 each on a horse (one horse each, the third led free), about an hour down at the party's very-heavy 6", about 8:40 pm at the basecamp. No dice, dungeon clock still round 131. Everything the party took from area 10 is now at the basecamp: all sixteen kilderkins (first eight JS2 RH1 TL1 XR1 Y1 Z2, second eight SD2 OG2 PF4), the four firkins, two casks, and the gear. 33 barrels remain in area 10.

## Day 123 -- dinner at the basecamp, about 8:45 pm
Ista cooks from the raw camp food and Dave keeps the party listening to her; eighteen mouths (party nine, Rowe, Orm, Coll, six hands), a third of a person-day each: six person-days off the stock (0.86 person-week). The sledge-way's first day is not reported beyond what is on record; Coll and the hands were not asked. No dice.

## Day 123 into 124 -- the night at the basecamp
Bed about 9:30 pm, first light about 6 am, no dice: the campaign rules out wilderness encounters, and nobody was asked for watches. Lean-to (sleeps eight): six hands, Orm, Rowe, Coll, who split it as they can; the party outside.

## Day 124 morning -- carts to Wethersby, ride to the watercourse
Two carts loaded to the 8,000 gp-wt call (DM figure, not a book's): cart 1 OG2 PF1 RH1 JS1 = 7,550 plus the Y firkin 390 = 7,940; cart 2 PF3 TL1 JS1 Y1 = 8,000. Eleven kilderkins (most that fit, lightest first). Left at the basecamp: SD2, XR, Z2 (11,900), firkins HU, TL, VW, casks B, QS. Rowe and Orm, four road lunches from the standard rations, unload at Wend's barn, return; arrive at dusk, back at the basecamp dusk day 125. Dave, Tristle, Frython rode to the watercourse: Durnhak d6=4, nothing new. Breakfast debited (0.86 person-week camp food).

## Day 124 -- up to the cave entrance with all the oil
All seventeen basecamp flasks (340 gp-wt) to the party, 20 in hand now. Whole party of nine; packed rate very heavy 6", so the half-hour outcrop takes an hour: about 9:15 to 10:15 am. No dice; the dungeon clock stays at round 131 of expedition 19 until they go in (expedition 20).

## Day 124 -- expedition 20, to the mouth of area 22
About 10:20 am at the cave mouth; 14 rounds to the corridor of area 22 (doors at (16,6), (14,5) and (10,2) passed and pulled shut behind, the one-way slab at (14,13) known). No dice fired. Party body at (10,5), Tristle 40 ft ahead at (10,9), the last square of the corridor and the mouth of the garden. Lantern topped off to 24 turns (broached flask first), Frython carrying it.

## Day 124 -- to (10,19), the north end of area 22
Round 19 of expedition 20, about 10:40 am. Along x=10 by the day-119 path (both ghouls dead, the trunks felled at (10,16) and (12,19), the platform at (11,19) probed). Nothing fired. The party body is at (10,19); Tristle cannot be 40 ft ahead here (the room ends), so he waits at the north wall. Lantern back with Ista. The five days since day 119 slumped the fallen caps to soft rot: colour only.

## Day 124 -- the oil laid in area 22, rounds 19-33 (NOT LIT)
North trail 91 ft (13 flasks) from the platform (11,19) via (10,19) down x=10 to (10,11); west trail 42 ft (6 flasks) from (5,11) to (9,11); the broached flask (0.64 pint) on the platform at (5,11). The two trails end ten feet apart at (10,11) and (9,11): NOT connected, and the player was told. Party ends at (9,12). No burn-time or spread ruling yet: the book gives none (rules/combat.md); to be announced BEFORE anyone lights. Oil pool: 27 sealed flasks, all in Wend's barn. Lanterns: Ista 22 turns, Dave's spare 24. Nothing fired on the clock.

## Day 124 -- the oil lit, rounds 34-38
Tristle lit the east end of the west trail (9,11) at round 36 and the south end of the north trail (10,11) at round 37, back at (10,10) at 38. Burn 3 rounds a foot, spread 10 ft a segment: west trail and platform burn out about round 39-40; the north trail's far end (11,19) lit about round 38 and burns out about round 41. DM call for the growth: wet fungus and mould smoulder and steam rather than burn; dry dead mats and any shelf fungus within a foot or two of the trail catch; puffballs near the trail burst in the heat and add spore cloud to the smoke (as day 119: blurred sight, coughing, no damage). Nothing else placed in the garden; no dice.

## Day 124 -- RULING, HIDDEN FROM THE PLAYER: what the fire did to area 22 (dice in rolls.tsv, declared first)
Module: the garden is thriving, "some strange magic ... keeps this incredible garden alive"; carpet of molds on floor, walls and parts of the ceiling; fungi of every kind. Severity d6 = 5: STRIPS PLUS PATCHES. Regrowth d6 = 6: NONE for days (the magic is slow; it does not undo a fire in an afternoon). Air d3 = 2: the garden's air is not fit to breathe until TWO HOURS after the oil dies (about round 41 + 120 = round 161); the corridor clears sooner (about an hour). Ghoul's rags at (11,11) d6 = 5: did NOT catch; the body is where it lay, scorched at most by heat.

WHAT IT LOOKS LIKE WHEN THEY COME BACK
- NORTH TRAIL: a black stripe a foot wide down the whole x=10 line from (10,11) to (10,19), the mold under it burned to a crust of ash, a scorched fringe 1-3 ft each side, the wet growth beyond blackened and slumped where steam blew out of it. Soot on the walls and ceiling above the line.
- PATCHES of dry growth burned out beside the trails, 10-20 ft across, bare grey stone showing through in each: (9,14)-(9,15) west of the trail; (11,17)-(12,18) round the stump and the fallen trunk, the trunk half burned and blackened, cap a puddle of charred pulp; the north end round the platform at (11,19) and the corner at (12,19); the west arm round (7,11)-(8,11) where the wrecked mushroom stump stood.
- PLATFORMS: the mold burned off both. (11,19) the same waist-high half-round of plain dressed stone the pole rapped on day 119, nothing on it or in it, black-streaked. (5,11) the same, plain stone, never opened or probed: nothing on it either (module: semicircular stone formations overgrown with fungoid matter, nothing more).
- WEST TRAIL: a black stripe from (5,11) east to (9,11), fringe scorched, thicker soot near the platform.
- The gap between (9,11) and (10,11) is untouched mold. The corridor growth is untouched, only smoke-stained.
- Giant puffballs near the trails burst, spore residue like grey flour over the burnt strips. Smell of burnt fungus and scorched cellar that lasts days. No living thing found, none stocked (both ghouls dead).
- Nothing revealed under the mold: no hidden feature here.

## Day 124 -- doors left OPEN on the retreat (player's correction)
The player: all the doors open behind the party; (10,2) is off its hinges (since day 119); (14,5) left open; the secret door (14,13,V) is spiked open. (16,6,H) taken as open too. Effect: open doors along the whole route from the garden to the cave mouth; nothing else changes. The smoke can drift out along the corridor.

## Day 124 -- Hask rides after the carts; the party helps Coll (about 11:15 am on)
Carts left the basecamp about 8:00 (my figure; earlier said only 'early morning'), 2.2 mph loaded. Hask on foot from the cave entrance to the basecamp about 40 minutes, mounts about noon, rides 6 mph (DMG light mount 60 miles a day): meets the carts about 2:20 pm on the made road, about 13.8 miles from the basecamp; passes the word ('bring back every flask in Wend's barn'; 27 sealed there, none in the carts), turns back and is at the basecamp about 4:40 pm. He did not have to stay in town. Party (eight) works two hours with Coll on the outcrop line, the work face about a third of the way up from the basecamp. No dice, no wandering ruling (outdoors).

## Day 124 afternoon -- the party works the rest of the day with Coll
Gear off in the lean-to (eight people, all but Hask; shared kit too). Lunch debited (15 mouths, 0.71 person-week). About 2:30 to 7:00 pm, eight people for four and a half hours, 36 man-hours; Hask back at the basecamp about 4:40 (his own choice whether he joins; his kit is on him). The party's labour: the crosscut saw and ropes from the lean-to are used. Total credit for the day beside the crew: about 6.75 man-days with the earlier two hours, about one crew-day; finish brought forward from the evening of day 129 to the morning of day 129. Wages: the crew draws a day less. No dice.

## Day 124 evening -- dinner and Tristle's ballet
Dinner debited (16 mouths, 0.76 person-week, camp food now 16.81). Wages for the sledge-way paid in full, 98 sp, at the player's word (supersedes my note that they would fall). Tristle's ballet for Coll: Dave and Torsat hum somberly, then sillier. No dice.

## Day 124 into 125 -- the night at the basecamp
Bed about 9 pm after the ballet, first light about 6 am, no dice (no wilderness encounters). Lean-to to Coll and the hands; party outside. Everyone at full hit points; no spells were cast on day 124. The garden's air is not fit to breathe until about two hours after the oil died (round 41 of expedition 20), a matter of dungeon-clock arithmetic for when they go back.

## Day 125 -- the party works the day with Coll; the carts come home at dusk
Hask's gear off in the lean-to (all nine now without kit). Breakfast and lunch debited (0.76 each, camp food 15.29). Nine people, about eight hours: nine man-days on top of the crew's seven. Sledge-way: about 36.75 of 49 man-days done at dusk; crew alone finishes the afternoon of day 127, ready day 128. Rowe and Orm arrive at dusk with the two carts and 27 sealed flasks, unloaded the eleven kilderkins and the Y firkin at the barn as told; L063's oil is at the basecamp. Dinner not yet debited. No dice.

## Day 125 evening -- dinner, dances, and Dave's ask
Dinner debited (18 mouths, 0.86 person-week, camp food 14.43). Dave invited anybody to dance; Ferd and Wat volunteered (colour only). Coll agreed to keep the crew for the hauling: the days the road saves are days already paid (seven working days in full), so the men haul on them for nothing more; past the seven, the same rate; outcrop only, never into the caves. Loop opened. No dice, no reaction roll (known NPC).

## Day 126 -- the sledge-way stands
Night quiet; day opens at first light. Party (nine, gear in the lean-to) works with Coll: 12.25 man-days left at dawn from 36.75/49, nine party man-days plus the crew's seven a day, so the road stands in about six hours, early afternoon. Breakfast and lunch debited (1.72, camp food 12.71). Coll pronounces it done; the wages (98 sp) fall due; the player has not paid them yet. Crew ready to haul from day 127 (L064). Yrtone fit at the Inn. No dice.

## Day 126 afternoon -- wages paid, Yrtone sets out, expedition 21 to the secret door of area 10
Dave paid Coll 98 sp (purse 2,176 gp 8 sp). Yrtone left the Inn on foot about one pm, alone (no horse in town, all three at the basecamp); a cold camp by the road; at the basecamp about midday on day 127. Party of eight geared up, four flasks (three sealed and the flask opened for the lantern: 19.2 turns of broached oil), rate 9" so forty minutes up the sledge-way (1:45 to 2:25); no smoke or smell at the cave mouth (the garden's air was fit two days ago). Into the caves about 2:30, eight rounds to the secret door at (9,25); Tristle at (9,25), the party body at (10,22). Elf passive rolled at both known secret doors. Nothing fired. Camp food 12.71, oil at the basecamp 23 sealed.

## Day 126 -- CORRECTION to the time on the trail (player's word)
The half-hour between the basecamp and the cave mouth is the time AT 9" (the pace the party has been keeping), not at 12" as I scaled it. So: 9" = 30 minutes, 6" = 45, 12" = 22 or 23. I had used 40 minutes at 9" and an hour at 6", so the day-123 and day-124 walks were narrated too long. Today: gear on 1:30, up 1:45 to 2:15 at 9", into the caves about 2:15, at the secret door about 2:25. Nothing else in the record changes: the dungeon clock is in rounds.

## Day 126 -- eight barrels rolled out to the cave mouth (expedition 21, rounds 10-37)
Picked by dice from the 26 sound (the player named none): B x2, TL, LC x2, HU, UT, AW. Left: 25 barrels, 18 sound and 7 slack: B2 PF1 TL1 Y1 Z2 QS2 OG1 HU1 JS1 SD1 RH1 AW1 UT1 MK1 EJ1 sound (no LC left sound), TL1 Y1 LC1 OG1 VW1 FT1 GG1 slack. DM weights (barrel 100 lb plus contents by the day-121 cask figures): B 270 lb, TL 290, LC 460, HU 237, UT 388, AW 400. Three noise checks, anchor 30 silent, nothing fired. About 3:20 pm at the mouth, lantern out. The party is at the cave mouth with eight barrels; the sledge-way is ready and the crew haul from here (loop L064).

## Day 126 -- Brand sent down; the party back to area 10 (expedition 22)
About 3:25 pm. Brand goes down alone, lightly kitted, at his normal 12": about 25 minutes, at the basecamp about 3:50, tells Coll and asks Rowe and Orm to help. Crew ruling (mine, for when it matters): four men and a brake rope to a sledge and a barrel; a run down about 40 minutes, the empty sledge back up about 30; nine men (Coll, six hands, Rowe, Orm) run two sledge teams, about 2 barrels per 70 minutes, so eight barrels in about four and a half hours. Brand is exempt from the shared kit (carry_exempt) while away. The party is seven and Tristle: seven rollers, so seven barrels a trip, not eight. Party in area 10 at round 15 of expedition 22, about 3:40 pm, secret door pulled shut behind, elf passive rolled at both known doors, nothing fired. Rate 6" with seven bearers.

## Day 126 -- the second trip, seven barrels (expedition 22, rounds 15-41)
Rations: the player said 'leave enough of the standard rations (back at the entrance)'; they are on the party, not at the cave mouth. I took 'the entrance' as the entrance of area 10, the room's secret door; three person-weeks (600 gp-wt against 555 over) left inside, rate 9" (450 ft/turn). If he meant the cave mouth the rations are simply booked to area_10 and can be corrected. Barrels picked by dice from the 18 sound: Z, AW, Y, MK, PF, B, RH (DM weights: Z 530 lb, AW 400, Y 160, MK 424, PF 330, B 270, RH 220). Left in area 10: 18 barrels, 11 sound (B1 PF0 TL1 Y0 Z1 QS2 OG1 HU1 JS1 SD1 RH0 AW0 UT1 MK0 EJ1) and 7 slack. Nothing fired; extras rolled quietly. About 4:10 pm at the mouth. Crew ETA at the cave mouth about 4:35.

## Day 126 -- rations left at the mouth; expedition 23 back into area 10
The player asked for the same amount left at the cave entrance as left near area 10: three person-weeks at the cave mouth, three inside area 10; the party keeps 3.81. Rate stays 9". Brand's whereabouts unchanged (basecamp; the player has not said he comes up). Round 11 of expedition 23, about 4:26 pm, in area 10, door pulled shut, elf passive rolled, nothing fired. Crew ETA at the mouth about 4:35: the 15 barrels and the rations stand there unattended for a few minutes, then the crew take the barrels.

## Day 126 -- third trip, seven barrels (expedition 23, rounds 11-37)
Rations by the door taken up again; QS, Z, HU, TL, OG, SD, UT picked by dice from the 11 sound (DM weights: QS 196 lb, Z 530, HU 237, TL 290, OG 400, SD 460, UT 388). Left in area 10: 11 barrels, 4 sound (B, QS, JS, EJ) and 7 slack (TL, Y, LC, OG, VW, FT, GG). Nothing fired. About 4:52 pm at the mouth. Brand came up with the crew (his instruction, as the player says) and waits at the mouth; resumed in carry_exempt. The crew arrived at the mouth about 4:35 and have started: two teams, the first two sledges left about 4:45 with 15 barrels then standing; not yet booked below the mouth.

## Day 126 -- expedition 24, back into area 10 with the rations
Rations at the mouth taken up (the party carries all 6.81 person-weeks); rate 9" with eight bearers. Round 11, about 5:07 pm, in area 10, door pulled shut; nothing fired. 11 barrels left: 4 sound (B, QS, JS, EJ), 7 slack.

## Day 126 -- the seven slack barrels mended (expedition 24, rounds 11-52)
Door spiked (two spikes, Dave, 10 -> 8). Repair ruled ordinary work, no roll: a barrel has twice a kilderkin's hoops, so two turns a barrel per worker; four workers (mallet, Ista's joiner's hammer, two hand-axe polls for drifts), 7 barrels in four turns. HAMMERING LOUD: one extra check a turn. **ROUND 32, TURN-2 EXTRA: d6 = 1, level 1 table d6 = 6, BERSERKERS. Count d2 = 1 (one berserker, hp 5). Approach d6 = 4 (declared first: 1-3 they come to the passage outside the secret door, 4-6 never near): NEVER CAME NEAR, THE CHECK IS SPENT, THE PARTY NEVER KNEW.** Turns 3 and 4 extras silent. About 5:55 pm at round 52. All eleven barrels sound: B1 QS1 JS1 EJ1 sound already, TL Y LC OG VW FT GG mended.

## Day 126 -- the fourth trip, eight barrels (expedition 24, rounds 52-80)
Spikes drawn (8 -> 10). Picked by dice from the 11: EJ, QS, VW, LC, OG, JS, Y, GG (DM weights: EJ 424 lb, QS 196, VW 376, LC 460, OG 400, JS 160, Y 160, GG 388). LEFT IN AREA 10: B, TL, FT, all sound. **ROUND 63 EXTRA (barrel noise): d6 = 1, level 1 table d6 = 3, KOBOLDS; count d6 = 4 (hp 4,3,3,2); approach d6 = 5 (declared first: 1-3 come to the passage outside the door, 4-6 never near): NEVER CAME NEAR, SPENT, THE PARTY NEVER KNEW.** (The other extra this trip and the round-32 berserker likewise spent.) About 6:15 pm at the mouth. Crew: two sledge teams from about 4:35: first pair left the mouth about 4:45, at the basecamp about 5:25, teams back at the mouth about 5:55, second pair left about 5:55; booked at the basecamp: two barrels chosen by dice from the first fifteen. 28 barrels at the mouth, two in transit (not booked).

## Day 126 -- the fifth trip, the three barrels that remained (expedition 25, rounds 0-28)
B, TL, FT, all sound, rolled to the cave mouth; the census containers (33 barrels, 16 kilderkins, 4 firkins, 7 casks) are all out of area 10. Nothing fired in this trip. About 6:50 pm at the mouth. Crew: second sledges reached the basecamp about 6:35 (OG and RH, chosen by dice). At the mouth 29 barrels and kilderkins are booked (two more in transit, not booked); the third pair leaves about 7:05. No dice besides the elf passive at the doors.

## Day 126 night -- TWO BERSERKERS AT THE CAVE MOUTH (expedition 26, round 90, about 10:30 pm)
Dinner at the basecamp (17 mouths, and I have miscounted: the party present was nine, so 18 mouths; a 0.05 person-week slip to correct). Dave asked Coll for two improvised sledges from green poles; Coll agreed, two by mid-morning day 127, four men each (the party's). Party back up at the cave mouth about 9 pm to guard the 29 barrels; three rest checks for the night. **Watch assumed: Dave and Torsat first (the day-119 pattern), Tristle and Ista second, Hask third; everybody else asleep, in armour, at the mouth shelf; nobody gave a watch order.** The rest call ran the whole three hours past the check at round 90; I corrected the clock to 90 and will charge the rest of the watch after this fight (my process error, again).
CHECK: extra (rest watch 1) at round 90: d6 = 1; level 1 table d6 = 6, BERSERKERS; count d2 = 2 (hp 5 and 4); approach d6 = 2 (declared first: 1-3 they come to the party at the cave mouth from inside the caves): THEY COME. Distance d3 = 2 -> 20 ft. Surprise: party watchers d6 = 5, not surprised; berserkers d6 = 2, SURPRISED, they lose 2 segments (the surprised side loses segments equal to its own die). Berserkers at MV 12 close 12 ft a segment. Dark corridor (16,2)-(16,3) north of the mouth; the watchers at (16,1). No morale for berserkers. hp 5 and 4, AC 7, two attacks each or one at +2 (their choice). The fight is logged in campaign/encounter.tsv. Awaiting the player's declarations for the two segments.

## Day 126 round 90 -- the berserkers die in the first surprise segment
Ruling applied (rules/combat.md, DMG p.62): the surprising side is the one NOT surprised, so the party got the 3x rate for bows at the ready: six arrows a segment each, Dave and Torsat (Dave hit twice with his six shots, damage 3 and 1; Torsat hit four times with his six, damage 2, 5, 5, 1). Berserker B (hp 4) took Torsat's 2 and 5; A (hp 5) took Dave's 3 and 1 and Torsat's 5. Both dead before they acted; no second segment needed. XP 29 (10 + 1/hp, 15 + 14). I narrated the pair as visible at 20 ft in a dark corridor; no darkness penalty was applied. Bodies at the corridor north of the mouth, unsearched (treasure type K individually, 20 sp earlier). Camp-food dinner corrected to 18 mouths.

## Day 126 night into 127 -- the watch resumed
The two remaining rest checks were placed by dice in an hour of the second window and a half hour of the third (rolls.tsv) and the rest was run in short blocks so nothing could be overrun: neither fired. First light about 5:30 am, round 510 of expedition 26. The party did not sleep a full eight hours (the fight broke the first watch), so no spells are recovered by that night's rest beyond what they had; nobody had spent any. Bodies unsearched.

## Day 127 -- the berserkers searched (expedition 26, rounds 510-515)
Tristle, Dave and Torsat, five rounds, the rest guarding. Treasure type K x2: eleven and ten silver, twenty-one in all (booked to the purse, coin). Weapons d6: 3 long sword, 1 broad sword (salvage for Mund). No armour: berserkers scorn it, AC 7 is none; I had narrated 'ragged mail' in the dark the night before, corrected to scraps of ring sewn on hide. All twelve arrows recovered. Ista's lantern topped off at first light and lit for five rounds. Nothing fired.

A junk row ('area 22, change x') was written to world_changes.tsv by a mistyped command on day 127 and removed as shell corruption, not an event (CLAUDE.md, day 103 precedent); it would have set area 22 to the state 'x'.

## Day 127 morning -- the party hauls with the crew's two sledges
Party down at 5:50, at the basecamp about 6:20 (30 min at 9"), breakfast (18 mouths, 0.86; camp food now 10.99, about four days at eighteen mouths). Coll's whole crew is on the two pole sledges (cut at first light, done about 10). The party takes the existing two (left at the basecamp overnight): teams Dave, Frython, Brand, Ista and Torsat, Blartho, Hask, Dellie; Tristle stays at the mouth with the barrels and rations. Each full run: 30 up empty, 10 loading, 40 down, 10 unloading = about 90 min; two runs by about 9:50; four barrels, chosen by dice: QS, VW, AW, B. The new sledges are ready about 10 am for the next runs. Yrtone at the basecamp about midday.

## Day 127 late morning -- third run, four sledges; Yrtone arrives
The rations: the player believed they were at the cave mouth; the carry log has ALL 9.81 person-weeks on the party since day 126 (the three left at the mouth and the three left in area 10 were both taken up again before the last trips). Nothing at the mouth. Told him. Third run: the crew's two new pole sledges (Coll's nine) and the party's two, sixteen men, four barrels a run: chosen by dice. Down at about 11:20; lunch with Yrtone arrived about noon (19 mouths, 0.9; camp food 10.09). Tristle at the mouth with a bow ready. Fourteen barrels and kilderkins sledged down in all today and yesterday; 19 at the mouth. Nothing else happened; the dungeon clock is not running.

## Day 127 afternoon -- runs four to seven; the rations mix-up
MY ERROR: my replies from the dawn report onward said the rations stood at the cave mouth with the barrels; the carry log had them on the party (picked up day 126, at the player's word). The player acted on what I wrote ('we pick up the rations, meant to do that awhile ago'). Owned up; log unchanged; memory saved. Hauling: after lunch (noon to about 12:30) four runs of four barrels with the four sledges, each 90 minutes: 12:30 to 6:00 pm, chosen by dice: LC GG Z PF / TL QS UT FT / B EJ B TL / B HU LC UT. Thirty barrels and kilderkins are now at the basecamp; three remain at the cave mouth: JS, TL, Y. Dinner not yet debited. Camp food 10.09 person-weeks. Nothing fired; the dungeon clock stands.

## Day 127 evening -- the last run
About 6 pm, dark (the player's word), Tristle alone at the cave mouth for about an hour and a half with his bow ready: HIDE IN SHADOWS d100 rolled and logged (30% is his figure), result NOT told to the player as asked; one rest check for the interval, silent, nothing fired. The party's sledges up empty (30 min), loaded (10), down (40) in the dark with lanterns; JS, TL, Y at the basecamp about 7:30; Tristle came down with the party. Every container from area 10 is below the cave mouth: the thirty-three barrels at the basecamp or with Wend, and the kilderkins split between the basecamp (SD2 XR1 Z2) and Wend's barn (eleven). Dinner about 7:45 (19 mouths, 0.9; camp food 9.19 person-weeks).

## Day 127 evening -- Dave's thanks
Ten gold pieces out of the purse, one each to six hands, Rowe and Orm and two to Coll, on top of the promised 98 sp (audit-skip). Played from the record: a bonus for work done lands (gifts fail, work lands, articles.md); Orm and Rowe's 'will not take pay' is pride, not plenty, and this is pay for the extra days, so they accept. No dice.

## Day 128 -- the caravan to Wethersby; the trio to the watercourse
Loaded by dice, then the largest container that still fitted (the player's earlier firkin-or-cask rule): cart 1 (mule) 7,900 gp-wt LC, HU, firkin VW; cart 2 7,900 MK, B, firkin TL, cask B; horse 1 4,700 B, kilderkin XR; horse 2 4,610 kilderkin Z, QS; horse 3 4,590 OG, firkin HU (carts at the 8,000 gp-wt call, horses at 5,000 less a 250 pack saddle). The caravan: Torsat, Blartho, Frython, Hask, Dellie, Ista, Brand, Coll and his six hands, Rowe and Orm driving; departs about 8 am, Wethersby about dusk; booked at Wend's barn on departure. Trio Dave, Tristle, Yrtone on foot by the goat line, about 9:20 at the watercourse: 'has Durnhak answered' d6 = 6 (declared first: 1-3 an answer waiting): NOTHING NEW. Back at the basecamp about 10:45. Thirty containers left in the lean-to. Camp food 7.39 person-weeks (breakfast and lunch debited). No wilderness encounters.

## Day 128 late morning -- corrections, and the trio push through to Wethersby
Player's points, both right. (1) Coll's hands brought their own picks, shovels, axes and a coil of rope on day 123 (days.tsv); they take them home with them; I was wrong to list them as staying. (2) The sledges were never on the record as Coll's: he was hired to build the road for sledges; the crew built two on the job and two improvised on day 127 morning, on the party's paid time, materials and ropes. They are the party's and stay at the basecamp: booked as asset 'sledge' x4 (weight a guess, 80 lb). The brake rope and the post at the steep pitch are fixtures of the road. Trio: camp food for lunch and dinner on the road (0.29 person-week; camp food 7.1); on foot, 21 miles, 0.8 to 1.3 days walking laden (overland.py), leaving about 11 am, at Wethersby about 10 to 11 pm after two meal stops; Dave's spare lantern (24 turns) for the dark, not tracked outdoors. No wilderness encounters.

## Day 128 night -- the Inn
Caravan at Wethersby about dusk (unloaded at Wend's barn, booked on departure), the seven at the Inn, the trio in about half past ten (walking laden, two meal stops, dark road, nothing on it). Rooms two to a room where the house has them, five rooms for ten; 10 gp at the house rate of 1 gp a person a night. A full night's sleep: Blartho, Dellie and Frython can re-memorise (none of them had spent a spell). Coll and the hands home; Rowe and Orm with the animals (no charge booked). No dice.

## Day 129 morning -- the caravan back to the basecamp; the trio in town
Seven of the party (Torsat, Blartho, Frython, Hask, Dellie, Ista, Brand) with Rowe and Orm, two carts and two mules, leave for the basecamp at first light, empty, at dusk there (booked on departure); the three horses stay with the trio in Wethersby. Lunch from the standard rations (9 mouths, 0.43) and dinner at the basecamp from camp food (0.43): rations 9.38 person-weeks, camp food 6.67. Trio: Toller bought the gold ring at 10 gp; Mund the broad sword at 5 and the long sword at 7 gp 10 sp (half list); a merchant's meal each at the Inn at noon. XP 22 by treasure realised (10 and 12). Purse 2,180 gp 2 sp. The plant is at the barn (cloth-making set). Wend not visited; Halm expected about day 130. No dice.

## Day 129 -- the RH cask; the trio ride to the watercourse
The RH cask: the carry log has it at the Inn since day 121 (unloaded there 'we'll take the casks with us to the Inn', opened with Holt, who passed on the seeds, resealed, kept against vermin); no row moves it to the barn. Told the player. The trio: sales took an hour, out of the Inn about nine, the noon Inn meal reversed (+3 sp), rations for lunch (0.14). Ride 21 miles at a light ride, about 6 mph: overtook the caravan about 10:40 am, some nine miles out, said hello and went on; the basecamp about 12:30, empty (Coll's crew went home); up the goat line on the horses about forty minutes to the watercourse, about 1:30 pm. Durnhak: d6 = 3 (declared first: 1-2 an answer waiting, a day after the last check): nothing new. No wilderness encounters.

## Day 129 afternoon -- the trio wait at the basecamp
Back down the goat line about 2:15 pm, the horses tethered; the fire and dinner started about half past five; the caravan (seven of the party, Rowe and Orm, two carts) in at dusk, about 6:45 pm, after 21 miles at cart pace. Twelve mouths at dinner from the camp food (0.57 person-week, camp food 6.53). Nothing on the record has been moved: the thirty containers, the sealed oil, the ropes, the sacks, the saw and the four sledges are all still at the lean-to. No dice.

## Day 130 -- everything loaded for Wethersby; no Halm
Halm did NOT set out (d6 = 4, declared first; audit-skip). Loads by dice, then the biggest that fits, then the sundries: cart 1 B, TL, cask QS, Y with the oil and a share of the rope; cart 2 PF, UT with the rest of it, the sacks and the crosscut saw; horse 1 Z kilderkin and QS (4,610); horse 2 LC (4,600); horse 3 SD kilderkin and HU (4,670). Everyone walks with the animals (ten of the party, Rowe and Orm), out at about 7:30, Wethersby about 5 pm at cart pace; the road empty of anything that would be Halm. Camp food 5.39 after breakfast and lunch. Nineteen containers and the four sledges stay. No wilderness encounters.

## Day 130 evening -- Halm asked in person
The party went to Halm's yard and asked him; he said yes at once (his standing want is a run, asked once on day 40; asked to his face it is work, not a gift). Terms: 8 sp a day for the man, cart and mule, written down by Wend in the morning; owed on his return. Cart, mule and Halm at the barn at first light on day 131. No dice, no reaction roll. Loop L061 noted.

## Days 130 night to 132 -- the second run to the basecamp, fast-forwarded at the player's word
Night at the Inn (10 gp). Day 131: Wend wrote Halm's hire at the barn at first light; three carts, three mules, three horses, ten of the party, Rowe, Orm and Halm out for the basecamp, 21 miles, at cart pace, there about 5 pm, a night at the lean-to. Day 132: loaded at first light by dice and biggest-that-fits: Halm's cart B and SD barrel; cart 1 AW, TL; cart 2 FT, RH, Y; horse 1 SD kilderkin and JS; horse 2 OG; horse 3 LC; out about 7:30, Wethersby about 5 pm. Eight barrels stay at the basecamp (AW, EJ, GG, TL, UT, VW, Z, Z: 33,260 gp-wt); four sledges; camp food 3.53. Rations 8.62. No wilderness encounters; nothing to interrupt. Halm owed 16 sp; the party has not paid.

## Days 132 night to 134 -- the last load
Halm paid 16 sp on day 132 (the player wrote 'Coll'; Coll was paid in full on day 126, so it is Halm's). The last load packed for the most: no barrel over 4,750 gp-wt goes on a horse and a Z (5,300) takes a cart alone with no room for another, so Z on Halm's cart and Z on cart 1, AW and GG (7,880) on cart 2, EJ, UT, VW on the horses: seven of eight; TL (2,900) has no carrier. Left at the basecamp: one barrel (TL), the four sledges, the last camp food (1.67). Out at first light day 133, the basecamp at dusk, loaded day 134 at first light, Wethersby at dusk. Halm owed 16 sp again on return. Rations 8; camp food 1.67. No wilderness encounters.

## Day 134 evening -- camp food, the oil, and Holt
Five more days of camp food bought for the eight who go back up (Rowe, Yrtone, Torsat, Blartho, Hask, Dellie, Ista, Brand): 40 person-days, 260 cp, 5.71 person-weeks (my reading of '5 more days'; not stated for how many). THE OIL: NOT at the basecamp. The 23 sealed flasks went on cart 1 on day 130 as 'sundries' under 'load everything we can' and are at Wend's barn; four flasks with the party; told the player. Holt agreed to come to the barn after the morning rush tomorrow to open and buy. The trip to the basecamp not yet run: waiting on the oil answer. No dice.

## Day 135 morning -- the second party goes back up; the trio wait for Holt
Rowe, one cart and mule, Yrtone, Torsat, Blartho, Hask, Dellie, Ista and Brand out at first light with the camp food (5.71) and all the barn's oil (the player: leave it at the basecamp); they arrive about dusk, use up the camp food there, load TL on day 136, and are home about dusk. Booked on departure. Carts and mules corrected: they had been left at the basecamp by the log; they are at Wethersby. Night at the Inn paid (10 gp). Lunch from the standard rations (0.38, rations 7.62). Trio: Dave, Tristle, Frython at the barn for Holt about half past nine. Orm stays with the animals. No dice. The rest of the trip (the load, the return) will be run after the Holt scene.

## Day 135 -- fourteen containers opened with Holt at Wend's barn
Dave carried the RH cask from the Inn to the barn (booked). Opened, one of each remaining mark: barrels AW (wine), EJ (fish in brine), FT (rye flour), GG (dill pickles), MK (salt pork), UT (hard candy); kilderkins JS (dried mushrooms), OG (water), PF (whole peas), XR (soft soap), Z (honey); firkins HU (raisins), TL (barley), Y (dried apples). Contents per the module d20 table. Condition: B1 area 10: stale but sound. RULING for a container whose hoops went slack and were driven home: d6, 1-2 ruined by air and damp. Dice: OG kilderkin d2 = 2, the mended one, condition d6 = 3, sound; PF kilderkin d4 = 1, a sound-hoop one; FT barrel (mended) d6 = 1: RUINED, musty, wormy, worthless; GG barrel (mended) d6 = 4: sound. Goods weights (lb, the DM figure less the cask): AW 300, EJ 324, FT 180, GG 288, MK 324, UT 288, JS 110, OG 150, PF 115, XR 150, Z 215, HU 34, TL 47, Y 14. PROPOSED prices, DM calls for the player to approve, fresh cp per lb: rye 2, barley 2, peas 3, dried apples 8, raisins 12, pickles 3, salt pork 8, fish 5, mushrooms 20, honey 20, hard candy 30, soft soap 6, wine 40 a pint (about a lb), water 0; stale factor 0.75 as for the day-121 goods. Holt takes everything but the water and the ruined rye. Nothing sold; nothing added to rules/prices.md until approved. Opened containers left unsealed draw vermin.

## Day 135 -- Holt opens the rest; the tally
Player approved the prices; logged in rules/prices.md. A CORRECTION to what I told him: the JS kilderkin holds 30 lb of goods, not 110 (2.25 gp, not 8.25), and liquids go by the pint (AW barrel 288 pints = 43.2 gp, SD barrel 32.4, SD kilderkin 16.2), not by the 300 lb I used. Mended containers opened: d6 TL barrel 4 sound, Y barrel 2 RUINED, LC barrel 2 CAKED (salt cannot spoil: sells at half), VW barrel 5 sound, SD kilderkin 5 sound, PF kilderkins 4 and 4 sound. Holt's till d6 = 2, x 25 = 50 gp in coin today. Tally of what is sound and wanted: about 419.2 gp at the stale prices. Not wanted: RH seeds, OG water, the ruined FT and Y barrels. Terms of payment are the player's to decide: 50 gp coin now, the rest in Holt's hand as he turns the goods (the SD precedent, L010), or the party sells elsewhere. Nothing sold yet; no XP until money changes hands.

## Day 135 -- the deal with Holt agreed; the XP
Deal: the sound goods 420.6 gp (the tally 419.18 plus the last TL barrel, 1.43, at the basecamp; sold in situ, the party brings it down), 50 gp coin now, the rest on a tab at the Inn plus 40 gp coin a month on the first market day; the 28 gp of day 71 folded in (L010 closed, L065 opened). XP awarded on the agreed price: 420 in encounters.tsv; xp.py divides treasure XP among the party (half shares Hask, Dellie, Ista), so each full share is about 56 before the +10%: Dave 924 to 985, Tristle 892 to 953 (NOT the level: I said it would carry him over 1,251 and forgot the division), Frython 840 to 901, Yrtone 836 to 898, Blartho 763 to 819, Torsat 760 to 816, Dellie 216 to 247, Ista 189 to 217, Hask 177 to 205, Brand unchanged. Held out of the sale: the RH seeds, the OG water, the FT rye and one Y barrel (ruined). The night at the Inn of day 134 was paid in coin (10 gp) before the deal: not credited. Inventory and carry rows for the sold containers written; assets holt_lot added.

## Day 135 -- while the eight are away: barrels, and Coll
Dave, Tristle and Frython: the ruined FT and Y barrels emptied (tipped on Wend's midden), scoured with river sand and hot water, left upside down with the bungs out to dry in the barn loft: about half a day's work for three; ruled no roll, the staves were sound and only the contents had gone. Booked as barrel_empty x2 (100 lb each). The heavy rope and the mortar given to Coll at the barn (his: work tools); Coll: no use for the water. The OG water (two barrels and two kilderkins) is still in its containers. Nothing fired.

## Day 135 afternoon -- the odds and ends sold
Water tipped into Wend's yard trough (two barrels and two kilderkins), stood up to dry: booked as barrel_empty and kilderkin_empty. Toller: comb 5, tapestry 40 (module values), perfume 3 a bottle (my figure), 51 gp; Mund: morning star and the gnoll sword as iron, 2 gp 10 sp each (half the list, halved again for the oversize), 5 gp; Holt: the keg 1 gp on the account. Purse 2,254 gp 3 sp. XP 57 (51, 5, 1) with the usual shares: Dave 994, Tristle 962. Nothing fired.

## Day 135 late afternoon -- Wend's news, from the offscreen ticks (eight delivered)
A001 tick 1 hold, tick 2 progress: ELLERY answered (author: he took the floor and the share at their word, heard the rumour and did not credit it). A002 tick 1 progress, tick 2 hold: OSK said YES (author: 5 sp a cart-day on the road with its mule, nothing standing; the sheet drafted, unsigned). A003 two holds. A004 tick 1 progress, tick 2 hold: she asked COLL first (author: he has no objection if the tenancy is written down and his crew is not asked to work Gyll's ground unpaid). Cuddy's tick (the church's man) is NOT told: it is his, not Wend's, and stays hidden until Cuddy, Yrtone or the hall says. Nothing money moved.

## Day 135 -- the Osk sheet accepted
Dave: 'we accept the terms'. L039 closed. The party's carts are hired to Osk at 5 sp a cart-day on the road only. Income is a hidden fortnightly tick (A018, d6: 1-2 one cart, 3-6 both; thirty or sixty silver a run; audit-skip), paid to Wend who keeps the purse; the money is booked from the purse when told, dated the day due (asset cart_hire). NOT SETTLED and told to the player: what happens when the party wants the carts on Osk's run days. Nobody has said. No dice.

## Day 135 evening -- the Inn
The trio's pints and beds go on Holt's tab: accounts.tsv rows, balance about 394 gp after the coin, the keg and tonight. The night's board and the ale are NOT coin. The eight are due at dusk tomorrow (day 136). The trio has a full night at the Inn; the next day opens when the player says.

## Day 136 -- the papers read against the barrels (L050, L011)
Frython, a day in Toller's shop. d100 = 18 vs 90 (75 + 15 for the decoded marks): success. Authored to the bounds the day-39 notes set and no further: the papers' provisioning shorthand IS the twenty barrel codes (so the mark on SD was the storekeeper's code, not a brewhouse: L011); the 86 marks stay unanchored and the counter unnamed, but the sealed undrawn stores make the marks worse, which the day-39 notes said they would. The journal's CONTENT stays shut. Nothing invented about what the marks mean or who made them. Agenda A007 closed. Not yet told to Holt.

## Day 136 late afternoon -- housekeeping and Petteia
L011 was closed on day 95 and Holt told on day 93; my day-136 note that he still needed telling was wrong and is corrected in the loop. L050 closed. Petteia: the player's addition, accepted: Holt keeps a set in the common room, added to people.tsv; no rules on the record, the player defines them. First game by a bare d6 each, Tristle 2, Dave 1: Tristle. No money staked. The eight are due at dusk.

## Day 136 dusk -- Petteia (renamed from the wrong spelling), and the eight come home
Player's correction: the game is PETTEIA; renamed everywhere. Best of three by bare d6s: Tristle 2 v 1, Dave 5 v 3, Dave 4 v 1: Dave 2-1. The eight (Rowe with the cart, Yrtone, Torsat, Blartho, Hask, Dellie, Ista, Brand) back at Wethersby at dusk with the last TL barrel (sold to Holt: not the party's), the oil and the rest of the camp food left in the lean-to at the basecamp. Their camp food debited (1.14). The night at the Inn is not yet decided or charged.

## Days 136 night to 137 -- hacksaws, the Inn, the road north
Two of the three hacksaws left at the barn. Night at the Inn on the tab (10 gp off Holt's account, balance 384.15). MIRROR: the silver mirror (the module's 90 gp, off the library orcs) was NEVER sold: no money row; it is still in the party's kit; the metal mirror is the working one (5 gp-wt). Toller would pay the module value. Durnhak added to the agendas (A019, weekly from day 129). README gets a section on offscreen.py status. Day 137: lunch for ten (10 sp), the three horses packed with the shared kit, all ten of the party walking, 21 miles, at the basecamp at dusk; dinner 0.48 (camp food 5.76). No dice.

## Day 137 morning -- the silver mirror sold before the road north
The player wanted the mirror sold to Toller before leaving town; I had already run the trip north in one go. It fits without an undo: an hour at Toller's pitch, out at about half past nine, still at the basecamp at dusk. Sold at the module's 90 gp at full value as with the bracelet, comb and tapestry. XP 90 shared. Purse and XP as computed.

## Day 137 dusk -- the slab: Durnhak has answered
Told from offscreen A019 tick 1 (day 136, progress). Authored to what Tristle's day-117 word asked (when and where; the 36 gp; help on the lower level): a meeting at the watercourse at noon on day 139, waiting until dark; the coin; the lower level to be talked about, not written. Trio out on the horses at about half past six, at the slab about 7:15, home by about 8 in the dark by the goat line; nothing on the way. The other seven made the camp and dinner. THE PARTY MUST BRING 36 GP: the purse is Wend's, in Wethersby; whether they have it in hand has not been checked; ask the player, do not assume. Agenda A019 stays open (ceiling 12) for his next word.

## Day 137 evening -- the working purse (audit-skip)
Standing rule (player): the party carries 100 gp when it goes north and balances it in town. Booked in carry.tsv as `coin`, a hundred gold pieces, against `party` (audit-skip); assets.tsv gives coin a weight of 1 gp-weight a coin, so the purse is 10 lb of load (shared kit 2,451 gp-wt, party rate unchanged at 9"). The ledger total is untouched: coin is a FOREIGN asset. The 36 gp for Durnhak on day 139 comes out of it. (audit-skip)

## Day 138 -- expedition 28, to the corridor of area 22
About 7 am, nine rounds from the mouth to the corridor. Party body at (10,5) with the lantern (30 ft light reaches (10,8)); Tristle at (10,9) in the dark on infravision (nothing warm). What is perceived from here: the smell of burnt fungus and scorched cellar, still strong at six days (burn was day 124 round 36); NOT the garden (the lamp does not reach it and infravision shows heat only). Untouched growth in the corridor mouth, smoke-stained. Nothing fired. The garden's damage is as ruled on day 124 (DM notes), unseen. Everyone with a missile weapon ready.

## Day 138 round 10 -- first look into the burnt garden, from (10,9)
Lantern 30 ft: they see (10,10) to (10,12). Ruling from day 124: the north trail is a black stripe a foot wide from (10,11) north, crust of ash where the mold burned, a scorched fringe 1-3 ft, wet growth beyond blackened and slumped, soot on walls and ceiling above; grey spore residue like flour from the puffballs near the trail. The gap between (9,11) and (10,11) is untouched mold. NOT visible from here: the ghoul's body at (11,11), the patches and the two platforms (all beyond 30 ft or round the corner). No regrowth (d6 = 6). Air fit. Nothing warm; nothing fired.

## Day 138 rounds 10-18 -- the walk round the burnt garden (no search)
Dave at (10,10) facing south with the second lantern (lantern-yrtone, 24 turns) on the floor beside him. The other nine walked the west arm to (6,12), back, north to (10,19), and back to (10,11), eight rounds, looking for living things only. d6 = 4 on 'has anything living moved in' (declared first: 1 yes): NOTHING LIVING. What they see: the black strips as ruled on day 124: north trail a foot wide down x=10, crust of ash; the patches of dry growth burned out to bare grey stone at (9,14)-(9,15), round the stump and the fallen trunk at (11,17)-(12,18), the north end and corner (11,19)-(12,19), and (7,11)-(8,11) in the west arm; the trunk half burned, the cap charred pulp; both stone half-rounds under no growth, plain dressed stone, black-streaked, nothing on them; the west trail a black stripe from (5,11) to (9,11); the gap at (9,11)-(10,11) untouched mould; the ghoul's body at (11,11) where it fell, its rags unburnt. NOT a search: nothing is found because nothing is looked for; nothing was there. Nothing fired.

## Day 138 round 91 -- THREE ORCS DURING THE SEARCH (expedition 28)
Anchor-90 check fired at round 91 (the search turn ran on to 98; I corrected the clock back to 91, my process error again: one step of 10 rounds spans an anchor). Level 1 table d6 = 1, ORCS; count d4 = 3 (hp 6, 4, 3; the list gave 6, 4, 3, 1); approach d6 = 3 (declared first: 1-3 they come): THEY COME, up the corridor from the south to Dave's lantern at (10,10). Distance d3 = 3 -> 30 ft. Surprise: party d6 = 3, orcs d6 = 5: neither. Orcs AC 6, HD 1, MV 9 (9 ft a segment), damage 1-6 or weapon, ML 8, infravision 60 ft (they see the lantern first). Dave awake guarding; the rest of the party at (12,11)-(12,12), about 20 ft from him. Awaiting declarations. Turn 8 of the search was in progress (round 88 to 98); turns 9 and 10 (west platform, north platform) still to do after.

Round 1 of the orc fight (round 91): initiative party 3, orcs 4: THE ORCS ACT FIRST. Two reached Dave at once (30 ft at 9 ft a segment, charging, two abreast in the 10-ft corridor): orc A missed (14 v 16), orc B hit for 6 (1d8; MM p.76 damage 1-8). Dave 9 -> 3. Then Dave fired two arrows: the first at A missed (3 v 14), the second at B hit for 4: B (hp 4) DEAD. The rest of the party rushed up behind him and changed weapons (no attacks this round). State now: orc A (hp 6) unhurt next to Dave, orc C (hp 3) behind it, orc B dead. Dave has 3 of 9 hp.

Round 2 (round 92): initiative party 1, orcs 2: THE ORCS ACT FIRST AGAIN. Both orcs' targets d3 = 2, 2: BOTH SWUNG AT YRTONE, both hit (18 v 16), 3 and 3: Yrtone 7 -> 1 OF 7 HP. Then Torsat missed orc A (7 v 14), Hask's spear from the second rank hit A for 1 (A hp 5), Yrtone missed C (10 v 14), Blartho's cure light wounds healed Dave 1 (d8 = 1): Dave 4 of 9. Dave changed to sword and shield this round (no attack). Front rank three abreast (Dave, Yrtone, Torsat), second rank Hask and Blartho. Orc A hp 5, orc C hp 3, both unhurt otherwise; orc B dead.

Round 3 (round 93): initiative PARTY 6 v 5, the party first. Dave (1, natural 1) and Torsat (hit, 2) at orc A: A hp 5 -> 3. Yrtone (4 v 14) and Hask (4 v 14) missed orc C (hp 3). Blartho's cure light wounds on Yrtone 1d8 = 5 (1 -> 6 of 7); Dellie's on Dave 1d8 = 1 (4 -> 5 of 9). Orcs then swung: A at Yrtone (d3 = 2), missed (15 v 16); C at Dave (d3 = 1), missed (5 v 16). MORALE: I had NOT made the check when the first orc fell in round 1 (my omission); made now, DMG p.67, ML 8 percentage 50: d100 = 73, BREAKS by 23: DISENGAGE AND RETREAT, from their next action. Blartho's spells: one cure light wounds spent today, both now gone? He had two cures and command; two cast, command left. Dellie's: two cures, one spent, one left.

Round 4 (round 94): party first (2 v 1). Dave missed (10 v 13), Torsat NATURAL 20 killed orc A (4 damage, hp 3). Yrtone and Hask missed C. Dellie's cure on Dave d8 = 7: Dave 9 of 9 (cap). The held missiles as orc C broke off: Frython's dart hit 1, Ista's natural-20 dart hit 1, Tristle's arrow hit 3: C (hp 3) DEAD. All three orcs dead. XP 43 (16 + 14 + 13), the usual shares: Dave 1,012, Tristle 980. Hit points: Dave 9/9, Yrtone 6/7, the rest full. Spells left today: Blartho command only; Dellie none; Frython sleep. The search of the garden stopped mid-turn 8 (round 91); the clock is at 95; turns 8 (the south-east outcrop, unfinished), 9 (west platform) and 10 (north platform) still to do. Bodies unsearched (orcs, treasure type to roll), Dave's two arrows in the corridor.

## Day 138 rounds 95-118 -- after the orcs
Dave, Torsat and Tristle gathered the missiles (no breakage rule: two arrows of Dave's, Tristle's arrow, the two darts, all back) and went through the three orcs: treasure L individually 7 + 9 + 5 = 21 ep (10.5 gp at the gnoll precedent, booked); weapons by d100 40, 33, 50: A axe and spear, B axe and spear, C axe and polearm (three axes, two spears, a polearm: salvage for Mund, left piled by the corridor wall unless the player takes them); no armour worth naming. The rest of the party finished the search: turn 8 to round 98, turns 9 and 10 to round 118. NOTHING FOUND in the garden: nothing is placed there (module: ghouls only; day-124 ruling: nothing under the mould). No rolls. Purse and XP as computed. Nothing else fired.

## Day 138 rounds 118-138 -- what the pits and the room were for (an inference, no dice)
Two turns, everyone, a pit a turn. Authored from the module's own words (once the showplace of the stronghold; sunken pits 10x20; semicircular stone formations overgrown; magic keeps it growing): the pits are stone-edged planting beds two feet deep, lip flush with the floor, packed dark earth under the mould (the burnt strips at (10,x) beside them let the party see the edge and the soil), no drain, no channel, no marks, nothing buried; the two platforms are raised beds or plinths; the floor between is flagstone under the carpet. The room was a pleasure garden. NOTHING HIDDEN, and nothing said about how the magic works or why it is still alive: unknown to the DM as well. Anchor 120 check rolled silently, nothing fired. Lanterns: Ista about 10 turns left, Dave about 11.

## Day 138 round 147 -- out at the cave mouth
Dave's lantern out at round 138 (11.2 turns left), the walk out nine rounds, Ista's lantern out at the mouth (9.3 turns left); about 8:55 am; nothing fired. The orcs' arms are still piled by the corridor wall in the dungeon, not carried. Expedition 28 is 147 rounds; the party at (16,1). Tomorrow: the meeting with Durnhak at noon at the watercourse (bring the thirty-six owed, the party carries a hundred in the working purse; audit-skip). Nothing else on the road.

## Day 138 -- the basecamp, the rest and the spells
Down at about 9:30 (half an hour at 9"). Yrtone's cure on herself: d8 = 3 for ONE missing point (7 of 7). Both lamps refilled from the pool (broached flask first, then a sealed one). A four-hour rest (about 9:45 am to 1:45 pm) and half an hour of prayer and study to about 2:15: the DMG p.40 four-hour minimum, as every earlier day. Re-memorised: Blartho cure light wounds x2 with command still in place; Dellie cure light wounds x2 with light still in place; Yrtone cure light wounds x1 (loadout cure x2, command x1); Frython's sleep was never cast. Lunch at the basecamp debited (0.48, camp food 4.8). Outdoors, so no wandering checks. Everyone at full hit points. The horses are grazing on their line. No dice beyond the cure.

## Day 138 afternoon -- expedition 29, to area 30
The oil: the carry log had FOUR flasks on the party but the light pool says 24 sealed (23 at the basecamp) and one broached: after refilling both lamps the party held two; corrected, then three taken: five in hand. THE ORC ARMS: the player said 'leave the orc weapons here' as if they were carried; they are NOT: I left them piled by the corridor wall of area 22 in the dungeon (booked nowhere). Said so. Up the sledge-way (30 min), in at about 2:50, seven rounds to the access room of area 30 at (22,9): Tristle inside at the wrecked door, party body 40 ft back at (25,10). Area 30 (module): an access room, empty, a descending stair that leads straight down into area 38 on the lower level: THE GNOMES' CAVERN (Durnhak's people). The party fought kobolds here on day 4 (exp 4). Tristle sees nothing warm; nothing fired. Ista's lantern lit, 24 turns.

## Day 138 -- the detour for the orc arms (expedition 29)
The player, at the moment of arrival at area 30: first area 22 for the orc gear, then area 30. Rewound: clock corrected to 0 and the party placed back at the mouth (my seven rounds toward area 30 struck). Eight rounds to the corridor at the mouth of area 22, two to gather three axes, two spears and the polearm (the arms rolled at d100 40, 33, 50: booked as owned now; 325 gp-wt of shared kit, the party rate stays at 9"), nine rounds back to (25,10): round 19. About 3:05 pm. Nothing fired. The orc arms go with the party into the caves this time: nobody has said to leave them anywhere.

## Day 138 round 23 -- the head of the stair down from area 30 (HIDDEN ROLLS, d6/d6/d100)
Presence d6 = 1: GNOMES ARE IN AREA 38 right now, working the old chamber (declared first: 1-3 present). In sight of the foot of the stair d6 = 4: no (only 1-2 in sight): so nothing warm seen. Tristle's Hear Noise d100 = 95 v 15: FAILED, he hears nothing. Reported to the player: only what a man sees at the head of a stair and hears: NOTHING. LEVEL 2 IS NOT EXTRACTED (mapdb): the game pauses to build it if the party descends; area 30's stair leads DIRECTLY into room 38 (module p.17). The meeting with Durnhak is tomorrow at noon at the watercourse; the party has NOT gone down. Nothing fired.

## Day 138 rounds 23-27 -- Tristle down the stair of area 30 to the foot (HIDDEN ROLLS)
CORRECTION TO MY OWN NOTE ABOVE: I wrote that level 2 is not extracted. IT IS: built and merged (mapdb/output/b1/b1.sqlite, level 2; stair square (20,14), area 38 below it, cells (18-24, 7-13); its extents are 'nearest numbered area' and unreliable, README p.721). Nothing was paused. The arms (3 axes, 2 spears, the polearm) laid in the SW corner of area 30, booked to a new place id area_30. Lantern-ista hooded fully at round 23 (it still burns), the party to (22,9), out of the line of the corridor. Tristle alone, no light, on elf infravision (60 ft: heat only).
Rolls: gnomes work by a lamp? d6 = 6 (declared first: 1-3 lamp): NO, they work in the dark on infravision, so there is no glow anywhere. Move Silently d100 = 4 v 30: SUCCEEDED, down and up. Heard if it had failed: d6 = 5 (1-2 heard): moot. Gnomes are present (round 23 roll) and none in sight of the stair foot, so infravision shows nothing warm. What he perceived at the foot: gritty loose stone underfoot, cold dry still air, a sense of a wide open dark (no wall near), and from far off a metallic tap, irregular, and a dragging, scraping sound; he cannot judge the distance. He SEES nothing: rubble, carts and timbers are cold and he has no light. Four rounds, round 23 to 27; nothing fired. The party did not learn where the gnomes are, that they are unlit, or how many.

## Day 138 round 27 -- RE-RULING of what Tristle saw at the foot of the stair (infravision)
The player pointed me to DMG p.59 (checked on the page render): openings in walls show plainly, materials may differ if generous, and outside hot or cold things infravision is about dark-cloudy-night sight. I had ruled 'nothing warm, so nothing seen', too strict. New ruling now in rules/exploration.md. The earlier scene STANDS (rolls unchanged: Move Silently 4 v 30, the gnomes unlit and none in sight) but what he PERCEIVED is corrected: at the foot he is at the north wall of a wide hall (about 60 ft across on the mapdb, extents unreliable): the walls, the corner and a narrower opening leading south; humped masses of rubble, and squarish low shapes he takes for carts, and long straight faint shapes (timbers) against the floor, wood reading fainter than stone. NOT seen: pickaxes, jacks, colour, floor detail, anything past the rubble piles that block a line. No warm figure in sight. Told to the player as a correction after the fact. The earlier burnt-garden scouting (10,9), day 138, was also run under the strict reading; the difference was only the shape of the room in the dark 60 ft ahead, and it stays as played.

## Day 138 rounds 27-30 -- the whole party down the stair into area 38 (HIDDEN ROLLS)
Lantern-ista opened fully at round 27 (21.3 turns). The party of ten down in three rounds, standard formation with Tristle second rank, and placed at (20,13), the stair foot at the north wall of the hall, level 2 (mapdb has no walk route between levels; placed, noted). 17 squares marked seen: the hall's north end, (19-23, 11-13), the stair, and (20-21, 10). Tristle in the light has no infravision (house rule: within about 30 ft of a lit lantern).
Rolls: do the gnomes notice the arrival? d6 = 5 (declared first: 1-3 yes): NO, the tapping goes on. Where they are: d6 = 1 (1-2 south passage): SOUTH, out of sight, past the narrowing (20-21,10) toward (18-21,8-9), some 50-60 ft off, no line of sight through the rubble; nobody warm in the light. They have not seen the light. Every gnome in area 38 is unaware; none has spoken. Reported: the lit ground round the foot (rubble, carts, timbers, jacks and picks under dust, module p.: no mining for some time, no signs of a recent camp at the stair) and the tapping and dragging, now placed 'somewhere south'.

## Day 138 rounds 30-34 -- east along the north wall of area 38 (HIDDEN ROLLS)
'Left' at the stair foot: the party faces south into the hall, so left is EAST (said so in the reply). Four squares, 40 ft at the exploring rate, to the corner at (24,13) where the north wall meets the east wall (x=25). Clock 30 to 34, no check fired. (The anchor-30 check fired at the moment of arrival on level 2 at round 30, d6 = 3: nothing, run at level 1's table by default; the chance is 1 in 6 either way.) Marked 11 more squares seen. Nothing found along the wall but rubble heaps, carts, timber and the tools.
Rolls: do the gnomes notice the party crossing the hall by lantern? d6 = 1 (declared first: 1-2 yes): YES. The gnomes in the south passage (some 50-60 ft off, no line through the rubble, but a lantern in an unlit hall lights the roof and heaps) HAVE SEEN THE LIGHT; the tapping and dragging STOP. They are watching from the dark on infravision and have not shown themselves, as they behave on the day-95 shaft (they do not come into the light unasked). Not yet decided: whether they call out or hold; roll for it on the next declaration (declare the table first). They have seen a party of ten and a lantern; they do not yet know who. Durnhak is not necessarily among them (he is due at the watercourse at noon on day 139).
Reported: dust, rubble, carts, timber, the two walls meeting in a hewn corner, and the sound stopping.

## Day 138 rounds 34-37 -- south along the east wall of area 38 (HIDDEN ROLL)
Three squares, 30 ft, to (24,10), the SE corner of the hall (the south wall at y=9 runs west from here for four squares with no opening; the east wall x=25 has none either; the way on south is the narrowing at (20-21,10) toward (18-20,9), 40 ft west and beyond the lantern). Clock 34 to 37, nothing fired.
Gnomes, declared table first (1-2 call out in gnomish, 3-4 hold and watch, 5-6 draw back out of the way): d6 = 5: THEY DRAW BACK. They withdraw from the lit hall by the south passage, away from the party, quiet (small feet, infravision, they know the ground). Reported: a faint scuff of small feet or a slide of stone from the dark to the west and south, moving off, then nothing. They are NOT calling out and NOT approaching. Where they go and whether they hold at a distance is undecided; roll when the party goes on.

## Day 138 round 38 -- Tristle greets the gnomes in gnomish (HIDDEN ROLLS)
Tristle, at (24,10), says hello in gnomish. Rolls, tables declared first: answer? d6 = 2 (1-5 one answers): A GNOME ANSWERS from the dark, from the south-west some 40-50 ft off, out of the lantern's reach. Durnhak among them? d6 = 4 (1-2 yes): NO. HOW MANY gnomes are in area 38 now: 1 + d4 (d4 = 2) = THREE (hp 7, 5, 4 from the module list; Durnhak's five work in turns). They are, by the record, Durnhak's own people (the five scavengers of area 38); Durnhak is not here and keeps the watercourse at noon on day 139 (his hide note of day 137 says of the lower level: 'there is work there and it is not a thing for a scrap, come and talk'; the party has come down unasked). NO reaction roll: known people, played from the record (wary, question for a question, they want how many / whether the party comes down / what it has taken, and they will not volunteer what they are doing). The one who answers: gruff, level, does not give a name or say whose he is; asks who greets them in their own speech and how many; they have SEEN ten and a lantern, so 'how many' is a test of honesty, not a real question. They have not stepped into the light. Tristle translates for the party (no common; the party has heard no gnomish of theirs but Tristle's).

## Day 138 round 39 -- the gnome tests Tristle's claim (no dice, authored from the record)
Tristle: 'Friends of Durnhak, ten of us; we have finished exploring the upper level and came down the stair to explore this one.' (His claim that the upper level is finished is his; I did not correct it.) The gnome answers as the record says they answer: a question for a question, non-committal. He does NOT confirm that he knows Durnhak's affairs (the day-137 hide note, the noon meeting) and does NOT say Durnhak is absent. He tests the claim: which of them has Durnhak spoken with, and what did Durnhak say to him. He still has not stepped into the light and has not said what the gnomes are doing. They will want, by the record: what the party has taken, and whether it is coming to work or to take (the 'what it has taken' concern is one they will not explain). Round 39, nothing fired.

## Day 139-ish round 40 (still day 138) -- the gnome's next question (HIDDEN ROLL)
Tristle names the deal (hauling metal, selling it, selling him supplies): TRUE, and it matches what the five know. Does the gnome show himself? d6 = 6 (declared first: 1-3 to the edge of the light, 4-6 he stays in the dark and asks): HE STAYS IN THE DARK. He concedes the point in one line ('that much is so', not more) and asks the question the record says they care about: what else the party has carried out of the halls above (what it has taken), a question he will not explain. No reason given, no name, no count of his own people. Round 40, nothing fired.

## Round 41 (day 138) -- Tristle declines the gnome's question (no dice)
Tristle will discuss what has been taken only with Durnhak's friend present, and with Durnhak. The gnome takes it without heat (it is a fair answer and a gnome's own way). He tells the party ONE thing: Durnhak is not in this hall (true; nobody is hurt by it, and the party knows Durnhak is to be at the watercourse at noon tomorrow). He then asks, as the record has Durnhak's own note: if it is a thing to be talked over with Durnhak, WHY come down before the talking. He has still not shown himself, named himself or given the gnomes' number. Round 41, nothing fired.

## Round 42 (day 138) -- the gnome shows himself (HIDDEN ROLL)
Tristle: the party has finished the floor above, claims no ownership, asks passage, offers help against things the gnomes would rather were dealt with, proposes an arrangement. Does the gnome show himself? d6 = 2 (declared first: 1-3 to the edge of the light): YES. One gnome steps to the edge of the lantern's reach at the SW, about 30 ft west of (24,10) along the south wall, by a rubble heap; the other two stay in the dark and unseen (three in all, hp 7, 5, 4; which of them this is I do not say). He is about three feet tall, leathery brown, a big nose, grey-white hair and a short beard, a leather cap and apron, a pick carried over one shoulder, a short knife at the belt. He speaks for himself only: 'We own no stone here; no man owns stone.' Passage is not his to grant or refuse; Durnhak makes the bargains, not he. He does NOT take up the offer of monsters (he says nothing of them: the record has him not volunteering) and does not say what the gnomes are doing in the hall. He reminds Tristle that Durnhak asked for talk before feet, as a courtesy, and asks what he should tell Durnhak of a party that came down before noon. No dice on his words: authored from the record. Round 42, nothing fired.

## Round 43 (day 138) -- Tristle offers to go up if the gnome says the word (HIDDEN ROLL)
Tristle thanks him and offers to withdraw and meet Durnhak at the appointed place and time if the gnome says that is what the message meant. Declared table first: 1-3 he says the word, go up and wait; 4-6 not his word to give, do as you judge, but leave the iron and tools in this hall where they lie. d6 = 5: NOT HIS WORD. He gives no order either way ('I do not say the word; Durnhak says it'). He asks the one thing the record has had them caring about: that what iron and tools lie in THIS hall (the carts' ironwork, jacks, picks, the iron fittings) be left where they lie, because it is being worked. He does not say by whom or why. That is the concern behind 'what have you taken': the gnomes strip the old workings for metal and this hall is theirs to strip. It squares with 'no man owns stone' (the stone is nobody's; the iron is worked). Nothing else volunteered: the monsters, the other gnomes, the passage south. Round 43, nothing fired.

## Rounds 43-46 (day 138) -- the party leaves area 38
Tristle: 'Understood. We'll go upstairs and talk to Durnhak tomorrow; we wish you well.' The gnome, no dice, gives a nod and the gnomish farewell, and does not follow or call after them; he stays at the edge of the light beside the heap. They return by the way they came (7 squares, 70 ft, known rate, two rounds), up the stair to its head at (21,10) on level 1 (one more round). The party at the head of the stair, lantern open; nothing said about leaving the dungeon yet; the orc arms lie where they were left in the SW corner of area 30. The three gnomes remain in the hall; they have been told nothing of what the party took (Tristle did not answer); Tristle promised to talk to Durnhak on day 139 at noon. Nothing fired.

## Rounds 46-54 (day 138) -- out to the cave mouth with the orc arms
The arms (3 axes, 2 spears, the polearm) taken up from the SW corner of area 30 and booked back to the party; carry.py check OK, party rate unchanged. Standard formation, Tristle 40 ft ahead, 34 squares (340 ft) at the known rate, 8 rounds, round 46 to 54, by the way the party came (the doors already open or wrecked). Party at the cave mouth (16,1). Nothing fired, no check due in the stretch. Ista's lantern still burning open, 18.6 turns; nobody said to put it out. About 3:50 pm, daylight at the mouth. The three gnomes in area 38 are undisturbed and have not followed.

## Rounds 54-70 (day 138) -- back in to area 13
The party changed its mind at the cave mouth and went back in for area 13 (no purpose declared). 70 squares, 700 ft, 16 rounds at the known rate, round 54 to 70; standard formation, Tristle 40 ft ahead; the route passes the secret doors (10,24,V) and (14,28,H): BOTH ALREADY FOUND AND USED by the party (world_changes exp.7 and 8), so no elf passive roll is owed on doors already known. The anchor-60 check fired at round 61: d6 = 6, nothing; silent. The door of area 13 (10,40,H) is unspiked and unlocked (opened day 95, swings NORTH into the corridor), party in the room at (11,40) about 4:06 pm. The room is as left on day 95: the iron bars are gone (all but one went down the gnome shaft, day 96, and that one is at Coll's basecamp), the light rope, the wire, both crosscuts and the hacksaws are taken; left are the heavy chain, 13 shovels, 15 chisels, 8 mallets (one with Tristle), the vise, the two toolboxes, the box of pegs, the bench, the barrel of 60 unfletched arrows, the empty branded barrels, 32 ruined picks and 2 broken jacks. Nothing has been through it since (the corridor outside is quiet). No purpose declared, so nothing else resolved.

## Round 70-71 (day 138) -- CORRECTION to my description of area 13, and the pegs
The player caught it: what I told him on arriving in area 13 contradicted what I told him a few days ago. HE WAS RIGHT. Cause: I built the room from the day-95 world_changes row 'CONTENTS ONLY', whose LEFT list is a snapshot of that afternoon; inventory_log.tsv shows the chain, shovels, chisels, vise, both toolboxes, the bellows and the 60 unfletched arrows were TAKEN OUT the same day (rows 165-173) and SOLD TO MUND on day 100 (rows 272-280); the 28 bars went down the gnome shaft and one came out. WHAT IS REALLY IN THE ROOM: the eight mallets (left to make room for the arrows), the eleven empty barrels, the 32 ruined picks, the 2 broken jacks, the box of wooden pegs and the 10-ft bench, nothing else logged. Earlier answer (line 63813 of the session) was the right one.
The barrels: the record (loops L011 close, day 95) says all eleven are 'the same size as the SD ale barrel', which is assets.tsv 36-gallon (100 lb empty, rules/equipment.md, a DM call, 1,000 gp-wt). They can be rolled like the full ones or carried two to a barrel; each is 100 lb, not light for one man (a man's normal allowance is far below 1,000 gp-wt). Not yet asked for.
Pegs: taken, one round: pegs_box, weight a DM CALL 5 lb (50 gp-weight), booked to the party in inventory_log and carry.tsv, round 71. Nothing fired.

## Rounds 71-79 (day 138) -- mallets, the door, the eleven barrels of area 13 (HIDDEN ROLLS)
Eight mallets taken (inventory_log +8, carry to party; 80 gp-wt). The door of area 13 (10,40,H) shut and spiked FROM INSIDE: it swings north into the corridor, away from the room, so jamb wedges (the weaker method, rules/exploration.md), two iron spikes emplaced (world.py; carry.tsv -2 to fix a FAIL in carry.py check: the manifest had carried 2 more than the party then owned), a cloak along the lower edge. Quiet, no roll. The party is SEALED IN area 13 until it undoes it. Round 71 to 79, eight rounds; nothing fired.
Ista and Tristle examine the eleven barrels: 6 rounds. Marks in the record: VN, WT, LO, two worn past reading, SD; the other five were never written down: AUTHORED NOW as arbitrary two-letter house codes not in area 10's list (the area-10 marks are codes, not initials): KB, CN, HM, PA, TV. Order VN, WT, LO, worn-1, worn-2, SD, KB, CN, HM, PA, TV.
Condition d6 (declared first: 1-2 hoops slack, 3-5 sound, 6 stave split or rotted through): VN 6 rotted, WT 2 slack, LO 5 sound, worn-1 4 sound, worn-2 3 sound, SD 4 sound, KB 1 slack, CN 1 slack, HM 6 rotted, PA 6 rotted, TV 5 sound. => 5 sound, 3 slack (WT, KB, CN), 3 rotted (VN, HM, PA).
Residue d6 (1 sour-sweet, 2 sharp, 3 oily or greasy, 4 dry meal or dust, 5 salt crust, 6 nothing; SD is ale by the record): VN 4 dry meal, WT 4 dry meal, LO 5 salt crust, worn-1 5 salt crust, worn-2 4 dry meal, KB 6 nothing, CN 2 sharp, HM 3 greasy, PA 6 nothing, TV 2 sharp. So NEITHER 'LO' NOR 'VN' NOR 'WT' means what its letters seem to say: the marks are codes. Contents undecided beyond the residue class: the party will have to judge what a crust, a meal or a sharp tang was. Reported in those terms only.

## Rounds 79-109 (day 138) -- Ista and Tristle mend WT, KB and CN
Ordinary work, no roll (day 123 precedent): two turns a barrel per worker, so three barrels with two workers is three turns, 30 rounds; hammering loud, one extra check a turn. Rounds 79 to 109. Extras (silent): round 89 d6 = 4, round 99 d6 = 2, round 109 d6 = 6: nothing. Anchor-90 check fired at round 92: d6 = 6, nothing. WT, KB and CN are now sound; the rotted three (VN, HM, PA) stay rotted. About 4:45 pm. The party is still sealed in area 13 (door spiked, cloak on the sill).

## Rounds 109-133 (day 138) -- the eight empty barrels rolled out of area 13, TWO CHECKS FIRED (HIDDEN)
Spikes (2) and the cloak recovered, the door of area 13 shut behind (unspiked, unlocked); the eight sound barrels booked as barrel_empty (inventory_log +8 from area 13, carry.tsv to cave_mouth; WT, LO, two worn, SD, KB, CN, TV; the rotted three, VN HM PA, left). Marks meanings undecided. Standard formation, Tristle 40 ft ahead with the ten-foot pole, eight rollers (all but Ista with the lantern and Tristle); route 70 squares (700 ft) the known way, at ROLLING PACE 6 inch (30 ft a round, the day-126 precedent; 23 or 24 rounds) with one noise extra a block. No elf passive rolled: (14,28,H) and (10,24,V) were found and used by the party long ago (world_changes exp.7 and 8), and the passive is for doors not yet noticed.
I RAN THE CLOCK THREE EIGHT-ROUND BLOCKS AND TWO CHECKS FIRED IN THEM. CORRECTED (clock.py correct to 120) and each resolved at the round it fired:
ROUND 120, anchor 120 + 0: level 1 table d6 = 1, GIANT CENTIPEDES (1-2), count d2 = 1. The party at round 120 (330 ft along the route) was in area 8, the wizard's workroom, at (12,24): a pocket sealed behind shut secret doors, (14,28,H) and (10,24,V). It cannot be reached: SPENT.
ROUND 125, extra (rolling noise): level 1 table d6 = 1, GIANT RATS (MM 2-5), count 1 + d4 (d4 = 3) = FOUR (hp 4,3,2,1), approach d6 = 5 (declared first: 1-3 they come to the party's line, 4-6 never near): NEVER NEAR. The party was at (19,19) on the y=19 passage. SPENT, the party never knew. Round 133 extra d6 = 2: nothing. Nothing else fired.
